Theorem 222.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Given a organic algebra A over field F with convergence analysis conditions, the decomposition A = A_1 ⊕ A_2 yields invariant subspaces with dimension at most 621. The spectral radius satisfies ρ(T) ≤ 622/4.

Proof: We establish this via a spectral argument. Note that by the convergence analysis decomposition theorem, the relevant homology group H_621(S, ≠) vanishes for dimension 621. The operator on the ambient space provides the required extension, and the obstruction class in H^622(S, F) is trivial. QED.
